# Supporting types

Generated by `bun run docs:protocol`. Edit source schemas, then regenerate.

JSON Schema validates structure. `x-ace-constraint` names additional semantic rules that clients must enforce; Zod remains the full validator.

## AccessMode

[JSON Schema](schema/AccessMode.json), input validation.

Type: ["read-only","ask","auto-review","full-access"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"ask"
```

## AccessModeOffer

[JSON Schema](schema/AccessModeOffer.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| mode | yes | [AccessMode.json](schema/AccessMode.json) |  |
| native | yes | [PermissionMode.json](schema/PermissionMode.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "mode": "read-only",
  "native": "example"
}
```

## AccountBadgeInput

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountBadgeInput.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: One or two non-whitespace text graphemes, no emoji, at most 64 UTF-16 code units.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"AB"
```

## AccountDirectory

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountDirectory.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Absolute Unix or drive-qualified Windows directory.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"/example/home"
```

## AccountQuota

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountQuota.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| auth | yes | ["logged_in","logged_out","unknown"] |  |
| cursorSdkAuth | no | [CursorSdkAuth.json](schema/CursorSdkAuth.json) |  |
| observedAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| billingMode | no | ["api","subscription","unknown"] |  |
| plan | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| resetCredits | no | [QuotaResetCredits.json](schema/QuotaResetCredits.json) |  |
| usageNotice | no | object | {"properties":{"reason":{"type":"string","enum":["signed_out","unavailable"]},"message":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256},"observedAt":{"type":"number","minimum":0}},"required":["reason","message","observedAt"]} |
| windows | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"},"maxProperties":32,"x-ace-constraint":"At most 32 quota windows."} |
| blockers | no | object | {"default":{},"properties":{"overflow":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"limitError":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"},"homeUnavailable":{"type":"string","enum":["home_missing","home_unreadable","foreign_home","home_conflict"]}}} |
| usage | yes | object | {"properties":{"inputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"outputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"costUsd":{"type":"number","minimum":0}}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "auth": "unknown",
  "blockers": {
    "homeUnavailable": "home_conflict",
    "limitError": {
      "advisory": true,
      "remainingPercent": 9,
      "resetsAt": null,
      "usedPercent": 4,
      "windowDurationMins": 10
    }
  },
  "cursorSdkAuth": {
    "expiresAt": 1,
    "source": "environment",
    "status": "logged-in"
  },
  "observedAt": 8,
  "resetCredits": {
    "available": 4
  },
  "usage": {
    "costUsd": 0,
    "inputTokens": 2
  },
  "windows": {}
}
```

## AccountSummary

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountSummary.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| cliHome | no | string | {"maxLength":4096} |
| implicit | no | boolean |  |
| isDefault | no | boolean |  |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| isolation | no | `"unsupported"` |  |
| authMethod | no | ["browser","api_key","unknown"] |  |
| signedInAs | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| shortLabel | no | [AccountShortLabel.json](schema/AccountShortLabel.json) |  |
| badgeUsesInitial | no | boolean |  |
| badgeColor | no | [AccountBadgeColor.json](schema/AccountBadgeColor.json) |  |
| badgeIcon | no | [AccountBadgeIcon.json](schema/AccountBadgeIcon.json) |  |
| quota | yes | [AccountQuota.json](schema/AccountQuota.json) |  |
| availability | yes | [AccountAvailability.json](schema/AccountAvailability.json) |  |
| blockedUntil | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "availability": "logged_out",
  "cliHome": "example",
  "homeStrategy": "default_cli",
  "id": "example",
  "installationId": "example",
  "installationVersion": "example",
  "isDefault": true,
  "label": "example",
  "provider": "pi",
  "quota": {
    "auth": "logged_in",
    "billingMode": "unknown",
    "cursorSdkAuth": {
      "source": "environment",
      "status": "logged-out"
    },
    "observedAt": 1,
    "plan": "example",
    "resetCredits": {
      "available": 6
    },
    "usage": {
      "outputTokens": 1
    },
    "usageNotice": {
      "message": "example",
      "observedAt": 7,
      "reason": "unavailable"
    },
    "windows": {}
  },
  "shortLabel": "AB",
  "signedInAs": "example"
}
```

## AcpSessionSupport

[JSON Schema](schema/AcpSessionSupport.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| capabilities | yes | object | {"properties":{"resume":{"type":"boolean"},"imageInput":{"type":"boolean"},"planMode":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["resume","imageInput","planMode"]} |
| mcp | yes | ["http","stdio","unavailable"] |  |
| modelSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| modeSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| subagentSessions | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| coverage | yes | ["source_profile","generic","legacy"] |  |
| visibility | yes | `"limited"` |  |
| raw | yes | object | {"properties":{"json":{"type":"string","maxLength":2048},"truncated":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["json","truncated"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "capabilities": {
    "imageInput": false,
    "planMode": false,
    "resume": false
  },
  "coverage": "legacy",
  "mcp": "stdio",
  "modeSelection": false,
  "modelSelection": true,
  "raw": {
    "json": "example",
    "truncated": true
  },
  "subagentSessions": true,
  "visibility": "limited"
}
```

### Variant 11

Semantic rule: Either one task (requestId, task, role, provider) or tasks: [{ requestId, task, role, provider }, ...].

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"delegate_task"` |  |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| access | no | [AccessRequest.json](schema/AccessRequest.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| accountId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| effort | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","const":"inherit"},{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AgentEffort.json"}]} |
| options | no | [AgentServiceOptions.json](schema/AgentServiceOptions.json) |  |
| requestId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$"} |
| task | no | [MessageText.json](schema/MessageText.json) |  |
| role | no | [DelegationRole.json](schema/DelegationRole.json) |  |
| wait | no | boolean |  |
| waitMs | no | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":600000} |
| workspace | no | [AgentWorkspace.json](schema/AgentWorkspace.json) |  |
| estimatedLoad | no | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |
| machine | no | [MachineName.json](schema/MachineName.json) |  |
| project | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| tasks | no | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":64,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DelegationTask.json"}} |
| await | no | ["none","any","all"] |  |
| timeoutSeconds | no | [WaitTimeoutSeconds.json](schema/WaitTimeoutSeconds.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "access": "read-only",
  "effort": "high",
  "machine": "studio",
  "op": "delegate_task",
  "project": "relay",
```

Semantic rule: Exactly one of url or number is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"thread.link_pr"` |  |
| url | no | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":2048,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |
| number | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| repo | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

Example:

```json

  "op": "thread.link_pr",
  "url": "https://example.invalid/"
}
```

| number | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| all | no | `true` |  |

```json
}
```

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"mouse"` |  |
| event | yes | ["mouseMoved","mousePressed","mouseReleased"] |  |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |
| button | no | ["none","left","middle","right"] | {"default":"left"} |
| buttons | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":7} |
| clickCount | no | integer | {"default":1,"minimum":0,"maximum":3} |
| modifiers | no | integer | {"default":0,"minimum":0,"maximum":15} |

Example:

```json
{
  "clickCount": 3,
  "event": "mouseMoved",
  "kind": "mouse",
  "x": 2,
  "y": 9
}
```

### key

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"key"` |  |
| event | yes | ["keyDown","keyUp","char"] |  |
| key | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| code | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| text | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| modifiers | no | integer | {"default":0,"minimum":0,"maximum":15} |

Example:

```json
{
  "event": "keyUp",
  "key": "example",
  "kind": "key",
  "text": "example"
}
```

### scroll

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"scroll"` |  |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |
| deltaX | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |
| deltaY | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |
| modifiers | no | integer | {"default":0,"minimum":0,"maximum":15} |

Example:

```json
{
  "deltaX": 2,
  "deltaY": 1,
  "kind": "scroll",
  "x": 5,
  "y": 4
}
```

### touch

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"touch"` |  |
| event | yes | ["touchStart","touchMove","touchEnd","touchCancel"] |  |
| points | yes | array | {"maxItems":10,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"x":{"type":"number","minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000},"y":{"type":"number","minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000},"id":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9}},"required":["x","y","id"]}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "event": "touchStart",
  "kind": "touch",
  "points": []
}
```

### ime

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"ime"` |  |
| text | yes | string | {"maxLength":4096} |
| selectionStart | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":4096} |
| selectionEnd | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":4096} |

Example:

```json
{
  "kind": "ime",
  "selectionEnd": 5,
  "selectionStart": 2,
  "text": "example"
}
```

### pick

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"pick"` |  |
| pickerId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| values | no | array | {"maxItems":1000,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":4096}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "kind": "pick",
  "pickerId": "example"
}
```

## BrowserState

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserState.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| url | yes | string | {"maxLength":8192} |
| closed | yes | boolean |  |
| backend | no | ["embedded","headless","user"] |  |
| status | no | ["ready","paused","recovering"] |  |
| reason | no | string | {"maxLength":2048} |
| pageStateLost | no | boolean |  |
| loading | no | boolean |  |
| loadError | no | string | {"maxLength":2048} |
| permissionDenied | no | object | {"properties":{"permission":{"type":"string","maxLength":256},"origin":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}},"required":["permission","origin"]} |
| blocked | no | [BrowserOriginBlock.json](schema/BrowserOriginBlock.json) |  |
| activeTabId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| tabs | no | [BrowserTabList.json](schema/BrowserTabList.json) |  |
| downloads | no | array | {"maxItems":128,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/BrowserDownload.json"}} |
| pending_dialog | no | [BrowserDialog.json](schema/BrowserDialog.json) |  |
| annotation | no | object | {"properties":{"owner":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256}},"required":["owner"]} |
| recording | no | object | {"properties":{"by":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/BrowserTabActor.json"}},"required":["by"]} |
| features | no | array | {"maxItems":32,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":64}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "closed": true,
  "features": [],
  "loading": false,
  "reason": "example",
  "recording": {
    "by": {
      "kind": "agent"
    }
  },
  "threadId": "example",
  "url": "example"
}
```

| pageStateLost | no | boolean |  |

| canGoForward | no | boolean |  |

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

```json
  ]
}
```

## Capabilities

[JSON Schema](schema/Capabilities.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| permissions | no | [PermissionCapabilities.json](schema/PermissionCapabilities.json) |  |
| permissionModes | no | array | {"maxItems":256,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/NativePermissionMode.json"}} |
| accessModes | no | array | {"maxItems":4,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccessModeOffer.json"}} |
| approvals | no | ["interactive","sandbox-only","none"] |  |
| steeringMode | no | ["native","interrupt-restart","queue"] |  |
| forkMode | no | ["native","context-handoff","none"] |  |
| childControls | no | ["native","read-only"] |  |
| childFidelity | no | ["full","summary","placeholder"] |  |
| steer | yes | boolean |  |
| steerTiming | no | ["immediate","after_tool"] |  |
| launchOptions | no | array | {"maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["effort","serviceTier"]}} |
| interruptCascades | yes | boolean |  |
| resume | yes | boolean |  |
| fork | yes | boolean |  |
| forkSubagents | no | boolean |  |
| sessionOptions | no | boolean |  |
| forkPoints | no | array | {"maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["turn","item","end"]}} |
| subagentTranscripts | yes | boolean |  |
| backgroundTaskControl | yes | boolean |  |
| goals | no | boolean |  |
| backgroundVisibility | yes | ["full","partial","none"] |  |
| planMode | yes | boolean |  |
| tokenUsage | yes | boolean |  |
| imageInput | yes | boolean |  |
| attachmentInput | no | object | {"properties":{"format":{"type":"string","enum":["claude","codex","opencode","acp"]},"documents":{"maxItems":32,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","maxLength":128}},"embeddedContext":{"type":"boolean"},"maxInlineBytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":33554432}},"required":["format","documents","embeddedContext","maxInlineBytes"]} |
| rewindFiles | yes | boolean |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "attachmentInput": {
    "documents": [],
    "embeddedContext": true,
    "format": "claude",
    "maxInlineBytes": 8
  },
  "backgroundTaskControl": false,
  "backgroundVisibility": "partial",
  "childControls": "read-only",
  "fork": false,
  "goals": true,
  "imageInput": true,
  "interruptCascades": true,
  "launchOptions": [],
  "permissionModes": [],
  "planMode": true,
  "resume": true,
  "rewindFiles": true,
  "steer": false,
  "subagentTranscripts": false,
  "tokenUsage": true
}
```

## CatalogChanged

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| requestId | yes | string |  |
| entries | yes | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/CatalogEntry.json"}} |

| type | yes | `"catalog.changed"` |  |

## CatalogEntry

[JSON Schema](schema/CatalogEntry.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| kind | yes | [CatalogKind.json](schema/CatalogKind.json) |  |
| name | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| title | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| description | yes | string | {"maxLength":2048} |
| icon | no | string | {"maxLength":4096} |
| source | yes | object | {"properties":{"provider":{"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},{"type":"string","const":"ace"}]},"scope":{"type":"string","enum":["global","project","plugin","ace"]},"path":{"type":"string","maxLength":4096},"plugin":{"type":"string","maxLength":256}},"required":["provider","scope"]} |
| invocation | yes | [CatalogInvocation.json](schema/CatalogInvocation.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "description": "example",
  "icon": "example",
  "id": "example",
  "invocation": {
    "name": "example",
    "type": "plugin"
  },
  "kind": "agent",
  "name": "example",
  "source": {
    "provider": "ace",
    "scope": "global"
  },
  "title": "example"
}
```

## CatalogKind

## CatalogList

[JSON Schema](schema/CatalogList.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one of threadId, draft or workspace is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| draft | no | object | {"properties":{"draftId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"workspaceId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/WorkspaceId.json"},"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"instanceId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128}},"required":["draftId","workspaceId","provider"]} |
| type | yes | `"catalog.list"` |  |
| query | no | string | {"default":"","maxLength":256} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":512} |
| workspace | no | object | {"properties":{"workspaceId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/WorkspaceId.json"},"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"instanceId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128}},"required":["workspaceId","provider"]} |
| subscribe | no | boolean | {"default":false} |

Example:

```json
{
  "draft": {
    "draftId": "example",
    "instanceId": "example",
    "provider": "codex",
    "workspaceId": "example"
  },
  "limit": 8,
  "query": "example",
  "requestId": "example",
  "subscribe": true,
  "type": "catalog.list"
}
```

## CliOperation

[JSON Schema](schema/CliOperation.json), input validation.

### Variant 1

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"capabilities"` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "op": "capabilities"
}
```

### Variant 2

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"process.kill_all"` |  |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| provider | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| project | no | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| key | yes | string | {"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]{1,128}$"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "key": "r.IxiKAqy6jsXjtw__ktP.RChZv0pDEHdydHQ46a3N3i9cOPTp479afj2.x.Mm1T8kFJ-Y",
  "op": "process.kill_all",
  "project": "example",
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

### Variant 3

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"mcp.enroll"` |  |
| scope | yes | ["read","operate"] |  |
| ttlMs | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":86400000} |
| project | no | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "op": "mcp.enroll",
  "project": "example",
  "scope": "operate",
  "ttlMs": 3
}
```

### Variant 4

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"mcp.list"` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "op": "mcp.list"
}
```

### Variant 5

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"mcp.revoke"` |  |
| id | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |

Example:

```json
{
  "id": "example",
  "op": "mcp.revoke"
}
```

### Variant 6

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"thread.tree"` |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "op": "thread.tree",
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

### Variant 7

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"task.spawn"` |  |
| parentId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| request | yes | [DelegationRequest.json](schema/DelegationRequest.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "op": "task.spawn",
  "parentId": "example",
  "request": {
    "acpAgentId": "example",
    "instanceId": "example",
    "project": "example",
    "provider": "opencode",
    "requestId": "ZV0D",
    "role": "example",
    "task": "example",
    "workspace": {
      "base": {
        "ref": "example",
        "remote": "3Y9DKJIdz"
      },
      "kind": "new_worktree"
    }
  }
}
```

### Variant 8

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"task.status"` |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "op": "task.status",
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

### Variant 9

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"task.cancel"` |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| key | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$"} |

Example:

```json

  "key": "j86t",
  "op": "task.cancel",
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

## CliTaskStatus

[JSON Schema](schema/CliTaskStatus.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| state | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AgentTaskState.json"},{"type":"string","const":"unresponsive"}]} |
| generation | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| status | yes | [ThreadStatus.json](schema/ThreadStatus.json) |  |
| outcome | no | [DelegationOutcome.json](schema/DelegationOutcome.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "generation": 6,
  "outcome": {
    "before": 6,
    "outcome": "completed",
    "result": "example",
    "threadId": "example",
    "truncated": false
  },
  "state": "unresponsive",
  "status": {
    "on": "background_task",
    "state": "waiting"
  },
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

```json
}
```

### snapshot

Semantic rule: seq must equal view.seq; a thread view's itemSeqs holds the creation sequence of every item in itemOrder.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"snapshot"` |  |
| subscriptionId | yes | string |  |
| seq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| view | yes | [SnapshotView.json](schema/SnapshotView.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "seq": 7,
  "subscriptionId": "example",
  "type": "snapshot",
  "view": {
    "kind": "threads",
    "seq": 7,
    "threads": {},
    "window": {
      "before": null,
      "counts": [],
      "total": 9
    }
  }
}
```

### events

Semantic rule: throughSeq >= afterSeq; events are ordered with (firstSeq ?? seq) > previous seq and seq <= throughSeq.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"events"` |  |
| subscriptionId | yes | string |  |
| afterSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| events | yes | array | {"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DeliveryEvent.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "afterSeq": 0,
  "events": [],
  "subscriptionId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 6,
  "type": "events"
}
```

### progress

Semantic rule: throughSeq must be >= afterSeq.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"progress"` |  |

| afterSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |

```json

  "afterSeq": 1,
  "subscriptionId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 7,
  "type": "progress"
}
```

## DelegateTaskOperation

[JSON Schema](schema/DelegateTaskOperation.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Either one task (requestId, task, role, provider) or tasks: [{ requestId, task, role, provider }, ...].

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"delegate_task"` |  |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| access | no | [AccessRequest.json](schema/AccessRequest.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| accountId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| effort | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","const":"inherit"},{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AgentEffort.json"}]} |
| options | no | [AgentServiceOptions.json](schema/AgentServiceOptions.json) |  |
| requestId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$"} |
| task | no | [MessageText.json](schema/MessageText.json) |  |
| role | no | [DelegationRole.json](schema/DelegationRole.json) |  |
| wait | no | boolean |  |
| waitMs | no | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":600000} |
| workspace | no | [AgentWorkspace.json](schema/AgentWorkspace.json) |  |
| estimatedLoad | no | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |
| machine | no | [MachineName.json](schema/MachineName.json) |  |
| project | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| tasks | no | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":64,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DelegationTask.json"}} |
| await | no | ["none","any","all"] |  |
| timeoutSeconds | no | [WaitTimeoutSeconds.json](schema/WaitTimeoutSeconds.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "access": "read-only",
  "effort": "high",
  "machine": "studio",
  "op": "delegate_task",
  "project": "relay",
  "provider": "claude",
  "requestId": "review-on-studio",
  "role": "Review-0.9-build",
  "task": "Review the 0.9 build and list failing benchmarks"
}
```

```json
      "frames": {
        "median": 4,
        "worst": 8
      },
      "hitchMs": {
        "median": 5,
        "worst": 6
      },
      "hostLoad": {
        "median": 6,
        "worst": 2
      },
      "layoutShift": {
        "median": 4,
        "worst": 4
      },
      "refreshHz": {
        "median": 1,
        "worst": 8
      },
      "settleMs": {
        "median": 2,
        "worst": 8
      },
      "windowMs": {
        "median": 4,
        "worst": 7
      }
    },
    "runs": [
      {
        "confidence": "high",
        "verdict": "minor_hitches"
      }
    ]
  },
  "source": "screen-frames",
  "target": {
    "bundleId": "example",
    "kind": "window",
    "windowId": 7
  },
  "verdict": "smooth",
  "windowMs": 2
}
```

## InteractionRepeat

[JSON Schema](schema/InteractionRepeat.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| runs | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":5,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"verdict":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionVerdict.json"},"confidence":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionConfidence.json"}},"required":["verdict","confidence"]}} |
| metrics | yes | object | {"properties":{"refreshHz":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"windowMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"frames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"latencyMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"settleMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"effectiveFps":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"droppedFrames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hitchRatioMsPerS":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"layoutShift":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hostLoad":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"captureOverheadPct":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"longTaskMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hitchMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"}},"required":["refreshHz","windowMs","frames","hitchMs"]} |

Example:

```json

  "metrics": {
    "captureOverheadPct": {
      "median": 4,
      "worst": 6
    },
    "effectiveFps": {
      "median": 2,
      "worst": 3
    },
    "frames": {
      "median": 4,
      "worst": 3
    },
    "hitchMs": {
      "median": 4,
      "worst": 5
    },
    "layoutShift": {
      "median": 4,
      "worst": 3
    },
    "refreshHz": {
      "median": 0,
      "worst": 7
    },
    "windowMs": {
      "median": 3,
      "worst": 5
    }
  },
  "runs": [
    {
      "confidence": "high",
      "verdict": "inconclusive"
    }
  ]
}
```

## ItemsPage

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsPage.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| seq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| items | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Item.json"}} |
| itemSeqs | no | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string"},"additionalProperties":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"x-ace-constraint":"Plain JSON object with every own opaque ID, including __proto__, validated and retained."} |
| itemsBefore | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |

## ItemsWindowRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsWindowRequest.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one target; before + after + target is at most 200 items.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"items.window"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| aroundSeq | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| itemId | no | [ItemId.json](schema/ItemId.json) |  |
| turnOrdinal | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| before | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |
| after | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |
| wholeTurns | no | boolean |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "aroundSeq": 2,
  "before": 3,
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "items.window"
}
```

## ItemsWindowResponse

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsWindowResponse.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: itemSeqs holds the creation sequence of every item.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"items.window"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| seq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| targetSeq | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| items | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Item.json"}} |
| itemSeqs | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string"},"additionalProperties":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}} |
| itemsBefore | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| itemsAfter | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "itemSeqs": {},
  "items": [],
  "itemsAfter": 3,
  "itemsBefore": null,
  "requestId": "example",
  "seq": 2,
  "targetSeq": 2,
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "items.window"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/PortableHandoff.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| lossy | yes | `true` |  |
| policy | yes | ["recent-complete-items","conversation-first"] |  |
| sources | no | array | {"maxItems":8,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/HandoffModel.json"}} |
| since | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| excerpts | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"citation":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/HandoffCitation.json"},"text":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}},"required":["citation","text"]}} |
| omittedItems | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| history | yes | object | {"properties":{"type":{"type":"string","const":"items.page"},"tool":{"default":"handoff_read","type":"string","const":"handoff_read"},"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"before":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"limit":{"type":"number","const":50}},"required":["type","threadId","before","limit"]} |
| limitations | yes | `"Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments."` |  |

```json
  "excerpts": [],
  "history": {
    "before": 1,
    "limit": 50,
    "threadId": "example",
    "type": "items.page"
  },
  "limitations": "Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments.",
  "lossy": true,
  "omittedItems": 8,
  "policy": "conversation-first",
  "sourceThreadId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 3,
  "version": 1
}
```

Type: ["forbidden","preview_unavailable","preview_not_found","preview_owned_by_another_thread","preview_limit","preview_link_refused","preview_refused"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"preview_unavailable"
```

## PreviewGatewayStatus

[JSON Schema](schema/PreviewGatewayStatus.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"ace-preview.status"` |  |
| status | yes | integer | {"minimum":400,"maximum":599} |
| reason | yes | [PreviewRefusal.json](schema/PreviewRefusal.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "reason": "session_expired",
  "status": 402,
  "type": "ace-preview.status"
}
```

## PreviewLaunch

[JSON Schema](schema/PreviewLaunch.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Choose exactly one executable, or an attach URL without an executable or autoPort.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| name | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| command | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| runtimeExecutable | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| args | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":128,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":4096}} |
| cwd | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| env | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| port | no | [PreviewPort.json](schema/PreviewPort.json) |  |
| autoPort | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| url | no | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":4096,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |

Example:

```json
{
  "command": "node",
  "name": "example"
}
```

## PreviewLaunchFile

[JSON Schema](schema/PreviewLaunchFile.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| configurations | yes | array | {"maxItems":64,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/PreviewLaunch.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "configurations": []
}
```

## PreviewLink

[JSON Schema](schema/PreviewLink.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| url | yes | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":4096,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |
| sessionMs | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |

Example:

```json

  "sessionMs": 9,
  "url": "https://example.invalid/"
}
```

## PreviewPort

[JSON Schema](schema/PreviewPort.json), input validation.

Type: integer. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
3
```

## PreviewRefusal

[JSON Schema](schema/PreviewRefusal.json), input validation.

Type: ["signed_out","session_expired","link_invalid","not_previewed","upstream_unavailable"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"signed_out"
```

## PreviewRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/PreviewRequest.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"preview.request"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| operation | yes | union | {"oneOf":[{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"list"},"suggestions":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["op"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"forward"},"port":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/PreviewPort.json"}},"required":["op","port"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"unforward"},"port":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/PreviewPort.json"}},"required":["op","port"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"link"},"port":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/PreviewPort.json"}},"required":["op","port"]}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "operation": {
    "op": "forward",
    "port": 2
  },
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "preview.request"
}
```

## PreviewResult

[JSON Schema](schema/PreviewResult.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"preview.result"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| ok | yes | boolean |  |
| error | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| previews | no | array | {"maxItems":64,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/PreviewDescriptor.json"}} |

```json
    "sessionMs": 1,
    "url": "https://example.invalid/"
  },
  "ok": true,
  "previews": [],
  "requestId": "example",
  "suggestions": [],
  "type": "preview.result"
}
```

## ProjectCloneUrl

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectCloneUrl.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: GitHub owner/repo shorthand, HTTPS, SSH or scp-style git@ URL with a host and repository path; no passwords, HTTPS usernames, whitespace or control bytes.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"git@example.invalid:repo.git"
```

## ProjectCommand

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectCommand.json), input validation.

### workspace.publish

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.publish"` |  |
| workspaceId | yes | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| input | yes | [RepositoryPublishInput.json](schema/RepositoryPublishInput.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "input": {
    "account": "r4IyZ4XpS",
    "description": "example",
    "host": "7x-kgu-o6szq5-5yi24lwqc-x9j.8eiasf9",
    "name": "ON",
    "owner": "vaWVmZ6E",
    "visibility": "public"
  },
  "type": "workspace.publish",
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

### workspace.add

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.add"` |  |
| path | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| name | no | [ProjectName.json](schema/ProjectName.json) |  |
| icon | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectIcon.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| create | no | `true` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "name": "&~%#y(${$~!",
  "path": "example",
  "type": "workspace.add"
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.root.add"` |  |
| path | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| create | no | `true` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "path": "example",
  "type": "workspace.root.add"
}
```

### workspace.create

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.create"` |  |
| parent | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| name | yes | [ProjectName.json](schema/ProjectName.json) |  |
| icon | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectIcon.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| git | no | object | {"properties":{"initialBranch":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":1024}}} |
| gitignore | no | string | {"maxLength":65536} |

Example:

```json
  "gitignore": "example",

  "parent": "example",
  "type": "workspace.create"
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.clone"` |  |
| parent | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |

## ProjectDefaults

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectDefaults.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: `model` is present only together with `provider`.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| worktree | no | boolean |  |
| access | no | [AccessMode.json](schema/AccessMode.json) |  |
| baseBranch | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":1024} |

Example:

```json
{
  "access": "full-access",
  "provider": "acp"
}
```

## ProjectDirectoryName

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectDirectoryName.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: A literal existing directory name; no forward slash or NUL. Host filesystem rules apply to backslashes.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"example"
```

| isGitRepo | yes | boolean |  |
| isProject | yes | boolean |  |

```json
  "isGitRepo": true,
  "isProject": false,
  "name": "example",

  "score": 0
}
```

## ProjectGlyph

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectGlyph.json), input validation.

Type: ["code","terminal","globe","database","cloud","robot","rocket","flask","book","cube","game","mobile","palette","gear","lightning","leaf","star","heart"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"leaf"
```

## ProjectIcon

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectIcon.json), input validation.

### Variant 1

Semantic rule: Absolute HTTP or HTTPS WHATWG URL, without ASCII tabs, CR, LF or trailing whitespace; at most 4096 UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce URL parsing in application code.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"https://example.invalid/icon.png"
```

### Variant 2

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"data:image/jpeg;base64,NXiAuaG="
```

```json
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| instance | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"} |
| enabled | no | boolean |  |
| defaultModel | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$","x-ace-constraint":"The pseudo model ID default is not accepted; choose a concrete model ID."},{"type":"null"}]} |
| binaryPath | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096,"pattern":"^(?:\\/\|[A-Za-z]:[\\\\/]).*$","x-ace-constraint":"Absolute executable path with no control bytes."} |
| customModels | no | array | {"maxItems":128,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"displayName":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"\\S"}},"required":["id","displayName"]},"x-ace-constraint":"Custom model IDs must be unique."} |
| hiddenModels | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| shownModels | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| hiddenGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| shownGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| favourites | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| showOnlyFavourites | no | boolean |  |
| hideDeprecated | no | boolean |  |
| maxCheckpointMb | no | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":256} |

```json
  "provider": "pi",
```

| progress | yes | [ProviderInstallProgress.json](schema/ProviderInstallProgress.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "progress": {
    "action": "uninstall",
    "lines": [],
    "method": "brew",
    "provider": "cursor",
    "sequence": 4,
    "session": "example",
    "state": "failed",
    "step": 2
  },
  "type": "provider.install.progress"
}
```

## ProviderInstance

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderInstance.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Native instances use native account IDs. ACP instances require matching agent, installation and instance references, id equal to instanceId, default_cli home strategy and an empty selector environment.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| implicit | no | `true` |  |
| managed | no | `true` |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| authMethod | no | ["browser","api_key","unknown"] |  |
| signedInAs | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| shortLabel | no | [AccountShortLabel.json](schema/AccountShortLabel.json) |  |
| badgeColor | no | [AccountBadgeColor.json](schema/AccountBadgeColor.json) |  |
| badgeIcon | no | [AccountBadgeIcon.json](schema/AccountBadgeIcon.json) |  |
| homeDir | yes | [AccountDirectory.json](schema/AccountDirectory.json) |  |
| env | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountEnvKey.json"},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountDirectory.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "env": {
    "CODEX_HOME": "/example/codex"
  },
  "homeDir": "/example/codex",
  "id": "codex-default",
  "label": "Codex",
  "provider": "codex"
}
```

| fork | yes | ["all","yes","no"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "account": "8jG0gaf7sm",
  "archived": "all",
  "fork": "all",
  "host": "6p8-941b13",
  "owner": "z0rN3iH7-c7",
  "query": "example",
  "visibility": "public"
}
```

## ReviewAnchor

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewAnchor.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Fingerprint lines count equals end - start + 1.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| position | yes | [ReviewPosition.json](schema/ReviewPosition.json) |  |
| revision | yes | [ReviewRevision.json](schema/ReviewRevision.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "fingerprint": {
    "after": [],
    "before": [],
    "lines": [
      "example"
    ]
  },
  "position": {
    "end": 1,
    "file": "example.ts",
    "side": "new",
    "start": 1
  },
  "revision": {
    "kind": "working-tree"
  },
  "state": "active"
```
```json
}
```

## ReviewFingerprint

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewFingerprint.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Total UTF-16 length of before, lines and after is <= 8192.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| before | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| lines | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":100,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| after | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| noFinalNewline | no | `true` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": [],
  "before": [],
  "lines": [
    "example"
  ],
  "noFinalNewline": true
}
```

| endedBy | no | ["steer"] |  |
| startedAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| endedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| error | no | [AgentError.json](schema/AgentError.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "agentId": "example",
  "checkpoints": {
    "after": "example",
    "state": "unavailable"
  },
  "endedBy": "steer",
  "id": "example",
  "permissionMode": null,
  "startedAt": 4,
  "state": "interrupted",
  "threadId": "example",
  "trigger": "parent_agent"
}
```

## ScreenAction

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenAction.json), input validation.

### click

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"click"` |  |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| button | no | ["left","right"] | {"default":"left"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "kind": "click",
  "x": 5,
  "y": 2
}
```

### type

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"type"` |  |
| text | yes | string | {"maxLength":4096} |

Example:

```json
{
  "kind": "type",
  "text": "example"
}
```

### key

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"key"` |  |
| keyCode | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":127} |
| modifiers | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":4,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["command","shift","option","control"]}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "keyCode": 7,
  "kind": "key"
}
```

### scroll

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"scroll"` |  |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| deltaX | yes | integer | {"minimum":-1000,"maximum":1000} |
| deltaY | yes | integer | {"minimum":-1000,"maximum":1000} |

Example:

```json
{
  "deltaX": 8,
  "deltaY": 5,
  "kind": "scroll",
  "x": 4,
  "y": 4
}
```

## ScreenCapabilities

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenCapabilities.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `2` |  |
| background | no | boolean |  |
| windowSelection | no | boolean |  |
| permissionEvents | no | boolean |  |
| maxSessions | no | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":8} |
| platform | yes | ["macos","windows","linux-x11","linux-wayland"] |  |
| capture | yes | object | {"properties":{"windows":{"type":"boolean"},"displays":{"type":"boolean"},"changeDriven":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["windows","displays","changeDriven"]} |
| input | yes | object | {"properties":{"pointer":{"type":"boolean"},"keyboard":{"type":"boolean"},"scroll":{"type":"boolean"},"text":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["pointer","keyboard","scroll","text"]} |
| uiTree | yes | boolean |  |
| semanticActions | yes | array | {"maxItems":8,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["press","focus","setValue","scroll","expand","select","performSecondaryAction","selectText"]}} |
| codecs | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["jpeg","h264"]}} |
| permissions | yes | object | {"properties":{"screen":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]},"input":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]}},"required":["screen","input"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "capture": {
    "changeDriven": false,
    "displays": true,
    "windows": true
  },
  "codecs": [
    "h264"
  ],
  "input": {
    "keyboard": true,
    "pointer": false,
    "scroll": false,
    "text": true
  },
  "maxSessions": 2,
  "permissionEvents": true,
  "permissions": {
    "input": "granted",
    "screen": "denied"
  },
  "platform": "windows",
  "semanticActions": [],
  "uiTree": true,
  "version": 2,
  "windowSelection": false
}
```

| op | yes | `"ui.act"` |  |
| sessionId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |

Example:

```json
  "action": "scroll",
```

| kind | no | [SearchKind.json](schema/SearchKind.json) |  |
| kinds | no | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":10,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/SearchKind.json"}} |

```json
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

## SourceControlPreferences

[JSON Schema](schema/SourceControlPreferences.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Hosts are unique and the selected host must be configured

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| hosts | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":32,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"host":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/GitHubHost.json"},"account":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/GitHubAccount.json"}},"required":["host"]}} |
| host | yes | [GitHubHost.json](schema/GitHubHost.json) |  |
| remote | yes | string | {"maxLength":100,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9._-]*$"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "host": "github.com",
  "hosts": [
    {
      "host": "github.com"
    }
  ],
  "remote": "origin"
}
```

## StorageItem

[JSON Schema](schema/StorageItem.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| kind | yes | [StorageKind.json](schema/StorageKind.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"maxLength":4096} |
| bytes | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| modifiedAt | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| threadIds | yes | array | {"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":128}} |
| disposition | yes | ["remove","protect"] |  |
| reasons | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":16,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/StorageReason.json"}} |
| forced | yes | boolean |  |
| branch | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","maxLength":1024},{"type":"null"}]} |
| removeBranch | no | boolean |  |
| effects | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":6,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["transcript_payloads","attachment_references","browser_data","worktree","local_branch","file"]}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "branch": "example",
  "bytes": 3,
  "disposition": "protect",
  "forced": false,
  "id": "example",
  "kind": "worktrees",
  "label": "example",
  "modifiedAt": 9,
  "reasons": [
    "expired"
  ],
  "threadIds": []
}
```

## StorageKind

[JSON Schema](schema/StorageKind.json), input validation.

Type: ["threads","attachments","artifacts","logs","worktrees","caches"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"caches"
```

## StoragePlan

[JSON Schema](schema/StoragePlan.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| revision | yes | string | {"pattern":"^[a-f0-9]{64}$"} |
| createdAt | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| expiresAt | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| retention | yes | [StorageRetention.json](schema/StorageRetention.json) |  |
| items | yes | array | {"maxItems":256,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/StorageItem.json"}} |
| bytes | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| complete | yes | boolean |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "bytes": 9,
  "complete": true,
  "createdAt": 1,
  "expiresAt": 5,
  "id": "example",
  "items": [],
  "retention": {
    "attachments": 1,
    "worktrees": null
  },
  "revision": "69239f0bf9525a5a9dadc238fa0b34254021f96982674a705d41eb547edf88ed"
}
```

## StorageReason

[JSON Schema](schema/StorageReason.json), input validation.

Type: ["retention_disabled","within_retention","archived_thread","not_archived","pinned","active_tree","pending_input","live_thread","referenced","leased","unowned","expired","task_finished","pr_merged","pr_closed","orphaned","missing_directory","main_worktree","locked_worktree","uncommitted","unpushed","unknown_git_state","unsafe_path","changed","already_removed","cleanup_in_progress","owner_unavailable"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"unknown_git_state"
```

## StorageRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/StorageRequest.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"storage.request"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| operation | yes | union | {"oneOf":[{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"storage.usage"}},"required":["op"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"cleanup.plan"},"kinds":{"minItems":1,"maxItems":6,"type":"array","items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/StorageKind.json"}},"retention":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/StorageRetention.json"},"forceItems":{"default":[],"maxItems":256,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256}}},"required":["op"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"cleanup.apply"},"planId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256},"revision":{"type":"string","pattern":"^[a-f0-9]{64}$"}},"required":["op","planId","revision"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"cleanup.status"},"planId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256}},"required":["op","planId"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"cleanup.compact"}},"required":["op"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"cleanup.compactAbandon"},"compactionId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256}},"required":["op","compactionId"]}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "operation": {
    "compactionId": "example",
    "op": "cleanup.compactAbandon"
  },
  "requestId": "example",
  "type": "storage.request"
}
```

## StorageResult

[JSON Schema](schema/StorageResult.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"storage.result"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| result | yes | union | {"oneOf":[{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"usage"},"usage":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/StorageUsage.json"}},"required":["kind","usage"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"plan"},"plan":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/StoragePlan.json"}},"required":["kind","plan"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"apply"},"apply":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/StorageApply.json"}},"required":["kind","apply"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"compaction"},"compaction":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/StorageCompaction.json"}},"required":["kind","compaction"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"error"},"code":{"type":"string","enum":["forbidden","busy","not_found","invalid_request","unavailable"]},"message":{"type":"string","maxLength":256}},"required":["kind","code","message"]}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "requestId": "example",
  "result": {
    "compaction": {
      "checkpointed": false,
      "databaseBytes": 7,
      "databaseBytesBefore": 0,
      "id": "example",
      "incrementalUnavailable": false,
      "reclaimableDatabaseBytes": 0,
      "state": "abandoned"
    },
    "kind": "compaction"
  },
  "type": "storage.result"
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| artifacts | no | union | {"default":30,"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":1,"maximum":36500},{"type":"null"}]} |

| idleThreads | no | boolean | {"default":false} |

```json
{
  "idleThreads": false,
  "logs": 7
}
```

## SubscriptionScope

[JSON Schema](schema/SubscriptionScope.json), input validation.

### threads

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"threads"` |  |
| window | no | object | {"properties":{"limit":{"type":"integer","minimum":1,"maximum":20},"project":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"archived":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["limit"]} |

```json
{
  "kind": "threads",
  "window": {
    "limit": 5,
    "project": "example"
  }
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"thread"` |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |

```json
{
  "kind": "thread",
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

{"oneOf":[{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"list"},"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"}},"required":["op","threadId"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"open"},"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"name":{"default":"Terminal","type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256},"cols":{"default":80,"type":"integer","minimum":1,"maximum":500},"rows":{"default":24,"type":"integer","minimum":1,"maximum":500}},"required":["op","threadId"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"subscribe"},"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"terminalId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"subscriptionId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"fromOffset":{"default":0,"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["op","terminalId","subscriptionId"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"unsubscribe"},"subscriptionId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128}},"required":["op","subscriptionId"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"write"},"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"terminalId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"data":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}},"required":["op","terminalId","data"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"resize"},"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"terminalId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"cols":{"type":"integer","minimum":1,"maximum":500},"rows":{"type":"integer","minimum":1,"maximum":500}},"required":["op","terminalId","cols","rows"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"close"},"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"terminalId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128}},"required":["op","terminalId"]}]} ## Thread

[JSON Schema](schema/Thread.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| pinned | no | boolean |  |
| pinOrder | no | number | {"description":"Place among pinned threads, higher first. Set on pin (the daemon's clock when the command names none, so a new pin leads) and cleared on unpin."} |
| listOrder | no | number | {"description":"Place in the Home list when it is ordered by hand (threads.sortBy manual), higher first. Absent: the thread's creation time stands in, so a new thread leads."} |
| unread | no | boolean |  |
| readAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| settledAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| settledReason | no | ["manual","inactivity","pr_merged","pr_closed"] |  |
| snoozedUntil | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| deletedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| activityAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| autoSettleAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| autoSettle | no | `false` |  |
| settleWhenDone | no | `true` |  |
| activitySeq | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| hasSentMessage | no | boolean |  |
| details | no | [ThreadDetails.json](schema/ThreadDetails.json) |  |
| live | no | [ThreadRunMetadata.json](schema/ThreadRunMetadata.json) |  |
| id | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| workspaceId | yes | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| title | yes | string |  |
| queue | no | [QueueState.json](schema/QueueState.json) |  |
| goal | no | [ProviderGoal.json](schema/ProviderGoal.json) |  |
| titleSource | no | ["provisional","provider","generated","person","agent"] |  |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| backend | no | ["acp","cursor-sdk"] |  |
| continuation | no | object | {"properties":{"state":{"type":"string","const":"read_only"},"reason":{"type":"string","const":"cursor_cli_retired"},"actionId":{"type":"string","const":"thread.continue_new"}},"required":["state","reason","actionId"]} |
| capabilities | no | [Capabilities.json](schema/Capabilities.json) |  |
| permission | no | [PermissionState.json](schema/PermissionState.json) |  |
| handoff | no | object | {"properties":{"sourceThreadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"truncated":{"type":"boolean"},"bytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["sourceThreadId","truncated","bytes"]} |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| effectiveCapabilities | no | [Capabilities.json](schema/Capabilities.json) |  |
| acpSupport | no | [AcpSessionSupport.json](schema/AcpSessionSupport.json) |  |
| rootAgentId | no | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| status | yes | [ThreadStatus.json](schema/ThreadStatus.json) |  |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| updatedAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| archivedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| imported | no | [ImportedProvenance.json](schema/ImportedProvenance.json) |  |
| lineage | no | [ThreadLineage.json](schema/ThreadLineage.json) |  |
| delegatedBy | no | [DelegatedBy.json](schema/DelegatedBy.json) |  |
| execution | no | [ExecutionSelection.json](schema/ExecutionSelection.json) |  |
| switch | no | [ThreadSwitch.json](schema/ThreadSwitch.json) |  |

## ThreadCatchUpRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/ThreadCatchUpRequest.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one of sinceSeq or sinceTime is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"thread.catchUp"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |

| sinceSeq | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| sinceTime | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |

Example:

```json

  "sinceSeq": 8,
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "thread.catchUp"
}
```

| listOrder | no | number | {"description":"Place in the Home list when it is ordered by hand (threads.sortBy manual), higher first. Absent: the thread's creation time stands in, so a new thread leads."} |
| unread | no | boolean |  |
| readAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| settledAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| settledReason | no | ["manual","inactivity","pr_merged","pr_closed"] |  |
| snoozedUntil | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| deletedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| activityAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| autoSettleAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| autoSettle | no | `false` |  |
| settleWhenDone | no | `true` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "activityAt": 5,
  "autoSettleAt": 1,
  "deletedAt": 5,
  "pinned": false,
  "readAt": 7
}
```

## UsageQuery

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageQuery.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: to >= from, their difference is <= 365 days, and groupBy entries are unique.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| from | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| to | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| groupBy | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":7,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"}} |
| filters | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"},"additionalProperties":{"minItems":1,"maxItems":50,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":8192}}} |

| orderBy | no | ["tokens","cost"] | {"default":"tokens"} |

| ref | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":1024} |
| remote | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[\\w.-]+$","x-ace-constraint":"A remote name: letters, digits, `_`, `.`, `-`; no leading `-` or `.`."} |
| head | yes | string | {"pattern":"^[a-f0-9]{40,64}$"} |
| fetch | no | ["fetched","unreachable"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "head": "1ffe1606659b46ddfd4ad5a832395e3b533e472c86301e01f5516c",
  "ref": "example"
}
```
